# Hallucination Review List

Found **1874** suspect blocks.

### book1_ch_01.json | P004_3
**RAW:** In both countries it was clearer than crystal to the lords of the State preserves of loaves and fishes, that things in general were settled for ever.

**EN:** In both countries, it was crystal clear to the nobles who monopolized the state's loaves and fishes (a reference to the biblical miracle, meaning government posts or privileges) that the current order of things was settled forever.

**KO:** 두 나라 모두에서, 빵과 물고기라는 국가의 전유물(성서의 오병이어 기적에서 유래한 표현으로, 국가의 관직이나 이권을 의미함)을 차지한 귀족들에게는 세상만사가 영원히 안정되어 있다는 것이 수정처럼 명백했다.

---
### book1_ch_01.json | P005_3
**RAW:** Mrs. Southcott had recently attained her five-and-twentieth blessed birthday, of whom a prophetic private in the Life Guards had heralded the sublime appearance by announcing that arrangements were made for the swallowing up of London and Westminster.

**EN:** Mrs. Southcott had recently celebrated her blessed twenty-fifth birthday, and a prophetic private in the Life Guards had heralded her sublime appearance by announcing that preparations were made for London and Westminster to be swallowed up.

**KO:** Mrs. Southcott은 최근 축복받은 25번째 생일을 맞이했는데, Life Guards의 어느 예언적인 사병이 런던과 웨스트민스터가 통째로 삼켜질 준비가 되었다고 선포함으로써 그녀의 장엄한 출현을 예고한 바 있었습니다. (참고: Mrs. Southcott은 18세기 말 영국의 자칭 예언자로, 당시 시대의 혼란상을 보여주는 인물입니다.)

---
### book1_ch_01.json | P006_1
**RAW:** France, less favoured on the whole as to matters spiritual than her sister of the shield and trident, rolled with exceeding smoothness down hill, making paper money and spending it.

**EN:** France, generally less favored in spiritual matters than her sister holding the shield and trident (Britannia, a symbol of Britain), rolled smoothly downhill, printing paper money and spending it.

**KO:** 방패와 삼지창을 든 자매(영국을 상징하는 브리타니아 여신을 의미)보다 영적인 문제에서 대체로 덜 축복받은 프랑스는 종이돈을 찍어내고 소비하면서 아주 부드럽게 내리막길을 굴러가고 있었다.

---
### book1_ch_01.json | P006_3
**RAW:** It is likely enough that, rooted in the woods of France and Norway, there were growing trees, when that sufferer was put to death, already marked by the Woodman, Fate, to come down and be sawn into boards, to make a certain movable framework with a sack and a knife in it, terrible in history.

**EN:** It is quite likely that, while that sufferer was put to death, there were trees growing in the woods of France and Norway, already marked by the Woodman, Fate, to be felled and sawn into boards. These boards would be used to build a certain movable framework equipped with a sack and a knife (the guillotine), which would become terrible in history.

**KO:** 그 수난자가 처형당할 때, 프랑스와 노르웨이의 숲에는 운명이라는 이름의 나무꾼이 언젠가 베어내어 역사상 끔찍하기 그지없는, 자루와 칼이 달린 어떤 움직이는 틀(단두대를 의미)을 만들기 위해 이미 표시해 둔 나무들이 자라고 있었을 가능성이 높다.

### book1_ch_01.json | P007_9
**RAW:** musketeers went into St. Giles’s, to search for contraband goods, and the mob fired on the musketeers, and the musketeers fired on the mob, and nobody thought any of these occurrences much out of the common way.

**EN:** Musketeers went into St. Giles's to search for contraband, the mob fired on the musketeers, the musketeers fired on the mob, and no one thought any of these events were particularly unusual.

**KO:** 머스킷총병들이 밀수품을 수색하기 위해 St. Giles's로 들어갔고, 폭도들이 총병들에게 발포했으며, 총병들도 폭도들에게 발포했지만, 아무도 이러한 사건들이 일상에서 크게 벗어난 일이라고 생각하지 않았습니다. (참고: St. Giles's는 런던의 대표적인 빈민가로, 당시의 무법천지와 일상화된 폭력을 보여줍니다.)

### book3_ch_02.json | P025_1

### book3_ch_06.json | P018_1
**RAW:** Because he had voluntarily relinquished a title that was distasteful to him, and a station that was distasteful to him, and had left his country--he submitted before the word emigrant in the present acceptation by the Tribunal was in use--to live by his own industry in England, rather than on the industry of the overladen people of France.

**EN:** Because he had willingly given up a title and social rank he disliked, and had left his country—he argued this was before the term 'emigrant' was used in the court's current sense—to survive through his own hard work in England instead of relying on the labor of the heavily burdened French people.

**KO:** 왜냐하면 그는 자신에게 불쾌한 칭호(title)와 불쾌한 지위를 자발적으로 포기했으며, 조국을 떠났기 때문이다. 현재 재판소에서 사용되는 의미의 망명자라는 단어가 쓰이기 전의 일이었다고 그는 변론했다. 그는 과도한 짐을 진 프랑스 국민의 노동력에 의지하기보다는 영국에서 자신의 노력(industry)으로 살아가기 위해 그렇게 했다.
